# Council Directive 81/645/EEC of 20 July 1981 concerning the Community list of less-favoured farming areas within the meaning of Directive 75/268/EEC (Greece)

**COUNCIL DIRECTIVE**

**of 20 July 1981**

**concerning the Community list of less-favoured farming areas within the meaning of Directive 75/268/EEC (Greece)**

**(81/645/EEC)**

<tr><td></td><td> COMMISSION DECISION of 27 July 1994 amending the boundaries of the less-favoured areas in Greece within the meaning of Council Directive 75/268/EEC</td><td> L 207</td><td>33</td><td>10.8.1994</td></tr>

**COUNCIL DIRECTIVE**

**of 20 July 1981**

**concerning the Community list of less-favoured farming areas within the meaning of Directive 75/268/EEC (Greece)**

**(81/645/EEC)**

### Article 1

The areas situated in the Hellenic Republic which appear in the Annex shall form part of the Community list of less-favoured farming areas within the meaning of Article 3 (3), (4) and (5) of Directive 75/268/EEC.

### Article 2

The Hellenic Republic shall forward to the Commission, within one year of the adoption of this Directive, a detailed communication indicating the time within which measures for the substantial improvement of the infrastructure will be effective in the areas referred to in Article 1.

### Article 3

This Directive is addressed to the Hellenic Republic.

## BILAG—ANHANG—ΠΑΡΑΡΤΗΜΑ—ANNEX—ANNEXE—ALLEGATO—BIJLAGE — ΜΕΙΟΝΕΚΤΙΚΕΣ ΠΕΡΙΟΧΕΣ ΚΑΤΑ ΤΗΝ ΕΝΝΟΙΑ ΤΟΥ ΑΡΘΡΟΥ 3 ΠΑΡΑΓΡΑΦΟΙ 3, 4 ΚΑΙ 5 ΤΗΣ ΟΔΗΓΙΑΣ 75/268/ΕΟΚ
